{"type": "tool", "tool": "write", "callID": "chatcmpl-tool-909a79c530d34f82", "state": {"status": "completed", "input": {"content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSession Audit & Dashboard Generator\nProcesses all AI coding tool sessions and generates reports + dashboard.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json, os, sqlite3, glob, json\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nOUTPUT_DIR = r'X:\\SESSION_ARCHIVES\\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\\session_reports'\nos.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\nall_sessions = []\n\n# ============================================================\n# 1. ANTIGRAVITY / ANTIGRAVITY-IDE (from existing catalog)\n# ============================================================\ncatalog_path = r'X:\\SESSION_ARCHIVES\\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\\catalogs_and_data\\sessions_catalog.json'\nwith open(catalog_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    catalog = json.load(f)\n\nfor s in catalog:\n    all_sessions.append({\n        'id': s['id'],\n        'tool': s['source'],\n        'date': s.get('first_ts', '')[:10],\n        'timestamp': s.get('first_ts', ''),\n        'title': s.get('initial_prompt', 'Untitled')[:80],\n        'summary': f\"{s.get('user_turn_count',0)} user turns, {s.get('assistant_turn_count',0)} assistant turns. Tools: {', '.join(s.get('tools_used',[])[:5])}\",\n        'status': 'Completed' if s.get('assistant_turn_count',0) > 10 else 'Partial',\n        'turns': f\"{s.get('user_turn_count',0)}u/{s.get('assistant_turn_count',0)}a\",\n        'recommendations': 'Review artifacts and consolidate learnings into project docs.',\n        'artifacts': s.get('tools_used', [])[:5],\n        'folder': s.get('folder_path', '')\n    })\n\nprint(f\"Antigravity sessions loaded: {len(catalog)}\")\n\n# ============================================================\n# 2. CHARM CRUSH (SQLite DB)\n# ============================================================\ncrush_db = r'C:\\Users\\karma\\.crush\\crush.db'\nif os.path.exists(crush_db):\n    try:\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(crush_db)\n        cursor = conn.cursor()\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\")\n        tables = [r[0] for r in cursor.fetchall()]\n        print(f\"Charm Crush DB tables: {tables}\")\n        \n        # Try to find session/conversation tables\n        for table in tables:\n            try:\n                cursor.execute(f\"PRAGMA table_info({table})\")\n                cols = [r[1] for r in cursor.fetchall()]\n                print(f\"  {table}: {cols}\")\n            except:\n                pass\n        conn.close()\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Charm Crush DB error: {e}\")\n\n# ============================================================\n# 3. CLAUDE (history.jsonl)\n# ============================================================\nclaude_hist = r'C:\\Users\\karma\\.claude\\history.jsonl'\nif os.path.exists(claude_hist):\n    with open(claude_hist, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        lines = f.readlines()\n    print(f\"Claude history entries: {len(lines)}\")\n    # Parse first few to understand format\n    for i, line in enumerate(lines[:3]):\n        try:\n            entry = json.loads(line)\n            print(f\"  Claude entry {i}: {list(entry.keys())}\")\n        except:\n            print(f\"  Claude entry {i}: parse error\")\n\n# ============================================================\n# 4. CODEX (sessions)\n# ============================================================\ncodex_sessions = glob.glob(r'C:\\Users\\karma\\.codex\\sessions\\**\\*.jsonl', recursive=True)\nprint(f\"Codex session files: {len(codex_sessions)}\")\nfor s in codex_sessions[:3]:\n    print(f\"  {s}\")\n\n# ============================================================\n# 5. The validation shows a few issues:

1. The `id` field in MASTER_INDEX.json contains the full filename including the tool prefix, so when we try to construct the report filename as `{tool}-{id}.md`, we get double prefixes like `charm-charm-crush-a3e81d0e-34c.md`. This is because the `id` field already includes the tool prefix from the filename.

2. There are 3 missing reports which are actually the non-report files: RECREATION_GUIDE, MASTER_INDEX, EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY - these are not session reports so that's fine.

3. There's one session with status `{session.get('status', 'Unknown')}` which is a template placeholder that wasn't filled in.

4. The tool extraction from filenames is also getting double prefixes sometimes.

I need to fix these issues:
1. The `id` should be just the session ID without the tool prefix
2. Fix the broken report files that have template placeholders
3. Update the dashboard generation to use correct report URLs

Let me fix the quick_reindex.py to properly extract the id and tool from the filename, and also fix the broken report files.

### part prt_05ba4561 (msg=msg_05ba3f0c) type=reasoning tool= @ 2026-09-01T06:24:51

I see the issue now. The validation script is comparing:
- Indexed IDs: `charm-crush-db66f4d7-b0a` (which includes the tool prefix from the filename)
- Report IDs: `crush-db66f4d7-b0a` (which is what the validation script extracts from the filename)

The problem is that:
1. The actual report filenames are like `charm-crush-db66f4d7-b0a.md`
2. The quick_reindex.py extracts `id` as `crush-db66f4d7-b0a` (everything after the first hyphen)
3. But the actual report filename is `charm-crush-db66f4d7-b0a.md`

So there's a mismatch. The report files have the format `{tool}-{sid}.md` where tool is `charm-crush` and sid is the UUID. But my quick_reindex.py is splitting on the FIRST hyphen only, which gives `charm` as tool and `crush-db66f4d7-b0a` as id.

I need to fix the parsing to handle the fact that the tool part can contain hyphens. The format is actually:
- `charm-crush-{uuid}.md`
- `antigravity-{uuid}.md`
- `antigravity-ide-{uuid}.md`

So I should split from the RIGHT side, not the left. Or better yet, I should extract the tool by matching known tool names.

Actually, looking at the actual filenames:
- `charm-crush-db66f4d7-b0a.md` - tool is `charm-crush`, id is `db66f4d7-b0a`
- `antigravity-ide-6e8f5a62-29d.md` - tool is `antigravity-ide`, id is `6e8f5a62-29d`

I need to split on the LAST hyphen(s) to get the UUID, and everything before that is the tool.

Let me fix the quick_reindex.py script.

### part prt_05ba735f (msg=msg_05ba6fcd) type=text tool= @ 2026-09-01T06:28:00

## Objective
- Audit all AI coding session history across OpenCode, Kilo, Antigravity, Charm Crush, Gemini, Qwen/FreeBuff, Claude Code, and Codex; produce per-session reports and an interactive dashboard with full chat access.

## Important Details
- User requires full documentation, backup of all sources, and dashboard with clickable summary/open-in-new-window full chat links for every session.
- All outputs stored under `X:\SESSION_ARCHIVES\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\session_reports\`.
- Full backup created at `X:\SESSION_ARCHIVES\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\session_sources_backup\` via robocopy.
- Source directories are read-only; no source files were modified.
- Charm Crush SQLite DB (`crush.db`) is 164.8 MB and was queried read-only for session metadata and messages.
- Some sources (Gemini protobufs, OpenCode binary data) identified but not fully decoded.

## Work State
### Completed
- Scanned all session sources and identified 1,561 sessions across 9 tools.
- Created full backup of all session sources to X: drive (2,090 files total, ~2.6 GB).
- Generated `MASTER_INDEX.json`, `MASTER_INDEX.md`, `EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY.md`, `DASHBOARD.html`, and 1,181 individual `{tool}-{session-id}.md` reports.
- Verified Kilo/OpenCode session history locations.
- Created `RECREATION_GUIDE.md` with exact scratch-recreation instructions and inline Python scripts.

### Active
- Extraction script encounters `FileNotFoundError` when `safe_sid` is empty for sessions with blank IDs.
- `MASTER_INDEX.json` (922.6 KB) and `EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY.md` are stale; last modified 2026-08-25.
- Dashboard needs rebuild to add clickable summary links and open-in-new-window full chat links.

### Blocked
- Full conversation content extraction from all 1,325 Charm Crush sessions times out when writing individual markdown files.
- `full_extraction.py` fails with `FileNotFoundError: [Errno 2] No such file or directory: 'X:\\SESSION_ARCHIVES\\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\\session_reports\\charm-crush-378b23a8-2e6.md'` due to empty `safe_sid`.
- Gemini conversation DBs require protobuf decoding for full content.
- OpenCode binary workspace data requires specialized extraction.

## Next Move
1. Fix `full_extraction.py` empty-ID bug and regenerate `MASTER_INDEX.json`/`EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY.md` from existing 1,181 reports using `quick_reindex.py`.
2. Rebuild `DASHBOARD.html` with clickable title links opening full chat in new windows.
3. If full Charm Crush extraction is needed, implement chunked writing or SQLite-backed report generation to avoid timeout.

## Relevant Files
- `X:\SESSION_ARCHIVES\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\session_reports\DASHBOARD.html`: interactive dashboard with search, filters, sort, expand, CSV export
- `X:\SESSION_ARCHIVES\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\session_reports\MASTER_INDEX.json`: full structured catalog of 1,561 sessions (stale, 2026-08-25)
- `X:\SESSION_ARCHIVES\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\session_reports\MASTER_INDEX.md`: human-readable index with per-tool tables (stale)

### part prt_0624858a (msg=msg_06248444) type=text tool= @ 2026-09-02T13:21:43

Suggestions for what to do next with the session archive vault:

1. **Add a per-tool breakdown view** — filter the dashboard by `charm` vs `antigravity` to compare the two sources' session counts, turn distributions, and completion rates.

2. **Surface "high-value" sessions** — flag the 136 Completed sessions (especially the antigravity ones with high turn counts like the 16u/672a "Wild Turkey" build) as a priority reading list.

3. **Tag by project** — most sessions lack project context. Run a quick pass extracting keywords/prompts and tagging them (e.g. "crypto", "AI tools", "music studio", "foot clan") so the dashboard search becomes project-aware.

4. **Add a source-folder link** — each report's `folder` field is empty. If you want, I can re-extract the source path for each session and add clickable "Open Source Folder" links to the dashboard, so you can jump from the report directly to the original `.crush`, `.jsonl`, or `.db` file.

5. **Backfill missing content** — ~963 Abandoned sessions have minimal content (2 messages, 0 tokens, "Fetch Analysis"). Worth investigating whether the Charm Crush DB stores deeper history we're not reading, or if these are truly empty.

6. **Generate per-project timelines** — merge sessions into project-level markdown dossiers (e.g. one doc each for Crypto Empire, Tellem Studio, Foot Clan, Quad Vision, ComfyUI) using prompt/title clustering.

7. **Add a 7-day trend chart** — the `by_date` data is available; a small chart showing session volume per day would reveal when you were most active.

Which of these would you like to pursue?

{"type": "tool", "tool": "write", "callID": "call_function_57amm55ygqqp_1", "state": {"status": "completed", "input": {"content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport os, sys, json, sqlite3, re\nfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nsys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')\nOUTPUT_DIR = r'X:\\SESSION_ARCHIVES\\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\\session_reports'\n\nprint(\"Loading master index...\")\nwith open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, 'MASTER_INDEX.json'), 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    sessions = json.load(f)\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(sessions)} sessions\")\n\n# === TASK 1: Per-tool breakdown ===\nprint(\"\\n=== TASK 1: Per-tool breakdown ===\")\ntool_stats = defaultdict(lambda: {'total': 0, 'completed': 0, 'partial': 0, 'abandoned': 0, 'turns': 0})\nfor s in sessions:\n    # Normalize tool name (strip -ide suffix, charm-crush -> charm, etc.)\n    tool = s['tool']\n    if tool.startswith('antigravity'):\n        norm_tool = 'antigravity'\n    elif tool.startswith('charm'):\n        norm_tool = 'charm'\n    else:\n        norm_tool = tool\n    \n    tool_stats[norm_tool]['total'] += 1\n    status = s['status'].lower()\n    if status == 'completed':\n        tool_stats[norm_tool]['completed'] += 1\n    elif status == 'partial':\n        tool_stats[norm_tool]['partial'] += 1\n    elif status == 'abandoned':\n        tool_stats[norm_tool]['abandoned'] += 1\n    tool_stats[norm_tool]['turns'] += s.get('turns_user', 0) + s.get('turns_assistant', 0)\n\nfor tool, stats in sorted(tool_stats.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]['total']):\n    pct_complete = stats['completed'] / stats['total'] * 100 if stats['total'] > 0 else 0\n    print(f\"  {tool}: {stats['total']} sessions, {stats['completed']} completed ({pct_complete:.1f}%), {stats['turns']} total turns\")\n\n# === TASK 2: High-value sessions ===\nprint(\"\\n=== TASK 2: High-value sessions (Completed + high turns) ===\")\nhigh_value = [s for s in sessions if s['status'] == 'Completed' and (s.get('turns_user', 0) + s.get('turns_assistant', 0)) >= 20]\nhigh_value.sort(key=lambda x: (x.get('turns_user', 0) + x.get('turns_assistant', 0)), reverse=True)\nprint(f\"Found {len(high_value)} high-value sessions\")\nfor i, s in enumerate(high_value[:15], 1):\n    total_turns = s.get('turns_user', 0) + s.get('turns_assistant', 0)\n    print(f\"  {i}. [{s['date']}] {s['title'][:55]} ({total_turns} turns)\")\n\n# === TASK 3: Tag by project ===\nprint(\"\\n=== TASK 3: Tag by project ===\")\nproject_keywords = {\n    'crypto': ['crypto', 'bitcoin', 'btc', 'eth', 'trading', 'whale', 'coinbase', 'binance', 'kraken', 'blockchain', 'defi', 'altcoin', 'token'],\n    'tellem_studio': ['tellem', 'studio', 'music video', 'song', 'beat', 'rx-7', 'rx7', 'drift', 'turbo', 'supra'],\n    'foot_clan': ['foot clan', 'footclan', 'jarvis', 'computer use', 'mcp'],\n    'ai_tools': ['ai tool', 'llm', 'model', 'claude', 'gpt', 'openrouter', 'ollama', 'comfyui', 'wan', 'flux'],\n    'antigravity': ['antigravity', 'google', 'extension', 'auth', 'login'],\n    'crypto_empire': ['empire', 'dashboard', 'portfolio', 'master'],\n    'quad_vision': ['quad', 'vision', 'browser swarm', 'playwright', 'consensus'],\n    'dll_media': ['dll', 'media', 'video take', 'storyboard', 'rx-3', 'rx3', '69rme', 'icb', 'brisbane'],\n    'tellemusic': ### part prt_0635fa7d (msg=msg_0635f8c0) type=tool tool=write @ 2026-09-02T18:26:48

{"type": "tool", "tool": "write", "callID": "call_function_slf7gxcrk26u_1", "state": {"status": "completed", "input": {"content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport os, sys, json, sqlite3, re\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\n\nsys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')\nOUTPUT_DIR = r'X:\\SESSION_ARCHIVES\\ALL_SESSIONS_DIRECTORY_VAULT\\session_reports'\nCRUSH_DB = r'C:\\Users\\karma\\.crush\\crush.db'\n\nprint(\"=== Backfilling Charm Crush sessions with full content ===\")\n\nconn = sqlite3.connect(f'file:{CRUSH_DB}?mode=ro', uri=True)\ncur = conn.cursor()\n\n# Build session_id -> full data map\ncur.execute(\"SELECT id, title, message_count, prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, created_at, updated_at FROM sessions\")\nsessions_raw = cur.fetchall()\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(sessions_raw)} sessions from DB\")\n\n# Build full message content for each session\ndef extract_text(parts_json):\n    \"\"\"Extract text from parts JSON\"\"\"\n    if not parts_json:\n        return ''\n    try:\n        parts = json.loads(parts_json)\n        texts = []\n        for p in parts:\n            if isinstance(p, dict):\n                if p.get('type') == 'text' and 'data' in p:\n                    texts.append(p['data'].get('text', ''))\n                elif 'text' in p:\n                    texts.append(p['text'])\n        return ' '.join(texts)\n    except Exception:\n        return ''\n\n# Get all messages\ncur.execute(\"SELECT session_id, role, parts, model, provider FROM messages ORDER BY session_id, created_at\")\nmessages_raw = cur.fetchall()\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(messages_raw)} messages from DB\")\n\n# Group messages by session\nsession_messages = defaultdict(list)\nfor msg in messages_raw:\n    sid, role, parts, model, provider = msg\n    text = extract_text(parts)\n    session_messages[sid].append({\n        'role': role,\n        'text': text,\n        'model': model,\n        'provider': provider\n    })\n\nprint(f\"Grouped into {len(session_messages)} sessions with messages\")\n\n# Now backfill the 1,178 indexed sessions\nwith open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, 'MASTER_INDEX.json'), 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    sessions = json.load(f)\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(sessions)} indexed sessions\")\n\nbackfilled = 0\nfor s in sessions:\n    if not s['tool'].startswith('charm'):\n        continue\n    # The id is truncated to 12 chars; match to full UUID\n    full_id = None\n    for db_id in session_messages:\n        if db_id.startswith(s['id']):\n            full_id = db_id\n            break\n    if not full_id:\n        continue\n    \n    msgs = session_messages[full_id]\n    if not msgs:\n        continue\n    \n    # Get first user message as full_prompt\n    user_msgs = [m for m in msgs if m['role'] == 'user']\n    if user_msgs and not s.get('full_prompt'):\n        s['full_prompt'] = user_msgs[0]['text'][:500]\n    \n    # Build summary from all messages\n    total_chars = sum(len(m['text']) for m in msgs)\n    models = list(set(m['model'] for m in msgs if m['model']))\n    providers = list(set(m['provider'] for m in msgs if m['provider']))\n    \n    # Store full conversation\n    s['full_conversation'] = msgs[:50]  # Cap at 50 messages\n    s['total_chars'] = total_chars\n    s['models'] = models[:5]\n    s['providers'] = providers[:5]\n    s['has_full_content'] = True\n    backfilled += 1\n\nprint(f\"Backfilled {backfilled} charm sessions with full content\")\n\n# Update reports for the high-value ones (longer messages = more content)\nprint(\"Updating high-content reports...\")\nupdated = 0\nfor s in sessions:\n    if not s.get('has_full_content') or not s.get('full_conversation'):\n        continue\n    \n    report_fname = f\"{s['tool']}-{s['id']}.md\"\n    report_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, report_fname)\n    if not os.path.exists(report_path):\n        continue\n    \n    # Only update if session has > 2 messages or > 500 chars\n    if len(s['full_conversation']) <= 2 and s.get('total_chars', 0) < 500:\n        continue\n    \n    # Build enhanced report\n    with open(report_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        existing = f.read()\n    \n    # Add conversation excerpt before \"## Recommendations\" if not already present\n    if '## Conversation Excerpt' in existing:\n        continue\n    \n    conv_section = \"\\n## Conversation Excerpt\\n\\n\"\n    for i, msg in enumerate(s['full_conversation'][:10]):\n        role = msg['role'].upper()\n        text = msg['text'][:300] + ('...' if len(msg['text']) > 300 else '')\n        conv_section += f\"**[{role}]**: {text}\\n\\n\"\n    \n    if '## Recommendations' in existing:\n        enhanced = existing.replace('## Recommendations', conv_section + '## Recommendations')\n    else:\n        enhanced = existing + conv_section\n    \n    with open(report_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write(enhanced)\n    updated += 1\n\nprint(f\"Enhanced {updated} reports with conversation excerpts\")\n\n# Save enriched in

### part prt_0b1c2ec6 (msg=msg_0b1c245b) type=text tool= @ 2026-09-17T23:45:36

## Review

Current outputs are a useful draft, but not yet authoritative:

- **1,178 sessions** indexed; **1,028** marked as having full content.
- **11 project timelines** generated.
- The current `DASHBOARD.html` is still the older build; the enhanced version was interrupted.
- **Data quality issue:** 1,058 unique IDs across 1,178 records, with 112 duplicate-ID groups and 70 duplicate-title groups.
- **Tagging is overbroad:** `crypto` appears on 1,040 sessions and `opencode` on 1,038 because generic words such as `token`, `code`, and `session` match almost everything.
- The Charm Crush backfill updated the index, but did **not** add conversation excerpts to the individual reports or dashboard.
- The trend calculation is polluted by duplicates and is actually the top 14 dates, not a true rolling 7-day trend.
- Source links point mainly to `crush.db` or raw folders; browser compatibility needs validation.

## Recommendation

Treat the current archive as a **draft**. Before publishing or relying on the timelines:

1. Canonicalize IDs from the full report filenames/source UUIDs and deduplicate records.
2. Normalize tools to `charm`, `antigravity`, etc.
3. Replace substring tagging with exact phrases and source-specific rules.
4. Generate per-session transcript files or add excerpts to reports.
5. Rebuild the dashboard from the cleaned index.
6. Validate every report/source link and regenerate timelines and trend data.

The next worthwhile step is a cleanup pass on the index, not further dashboard features.
